## What is Novig?
Novig is one of the fastest-growing peer-to-peer sports prediction markets. On Novig, users trade directly with each other without the "vig" that is associated with a typical sportsbook.
The vig represents the money that sportsbooks charge you to use their service. It's a hidden cost built into the odds offered on each side of a bet. When a book offers -110 odds on both sides of a bet, they're keeping close to 10% of every bettor's wager, regardless of whether you win or lose.
On [Novig](https://novig.onelink.me/JHQQ/nfg364h9), users trade with each other on the outcomes of sporting events, similarly to the way that investors trade on the stock market. But instead of having to beat the books, you just have to win against other users - there's no commission kept by the site.
Users can bet on most traditional sports markets, including moneyline bets, spreads, totals, player props, parlays, and futures! No politics, no betting on wars...just sports!
## NBA Prop Bet Picks
_All odds are from Novig and were accurate as of 6:00 AM ET. All odds listed are from Novig and shown as percentages, not traditional American odds._
**[Jaden McDaniels](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002553/Jaden+McDaniels) OVER 25.5 Points + Rebounds + Assists (50%)**
McDaniels is averaging 25.4 PRA through five games to open the 2026-2027 NBA season, and now [Anthony Edwards](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002523/Anthony+Edwards) will miss significant time. In his first game with Edwards out, McDaniels produced 19 points, 7 rebounds, and 4 assists for 30 PRA against Golden State.
Most notably, McDaniels' usage rate jumped from 16.1% in the first four games to 25% in his first game with Edwards sidelined. The 26-year-old forward attempted 18 shots from the floor and also played a season-high 35 minutes in regulation. He played over 36 minutes in the previous game, but two of those minutes came in the overtime period against Cleveland.
**[Brandon Miller](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003032/Brandon+Miller) OVER 37.5 Points + Rebounds + Assists (50%)**
Miller has had an insane role for Charlotte with [Coby White](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002280/Coby+White) and [Kon Knueppel](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003451/Kon+Knueppel) out, and now [Dennis Schroder](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000606/Dennis+Schr%C3%B6der) is also questionable. Miller has played more than 35 minutes in four of five games this season, and he's scored 30+ points in each of his last three. He posted a usage rate above 35% in those five contests.
Miller went off for 39 points, 6 rebounds, and 1 assist against Washington on Monday and gets that same matchup today. Washington ranks just 19th in defensive rating on the season. He is the clear number one option for this Charlotte team, and could be in line for increased playmaking opportunities if Schroder can't play.
In addition to the huge scoring totals, Miller has racked up 26 rebounds and 8 assists over his last three games. He is basically a one-man show for Charlotte right now.

## NBA Value Props
_All odds are from Novig and were accurate as of 9:00 AM ET. All odds listed are from Novig and shown as percentages, not traditional American odds._
**[Pelle Larsson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003245/Pelle+Larsson) OVER 3.5 Assists (55%)**
Larsson has already carved out a significant role in Miami, and his playmaking has been on full display. He's averaged 4.2 assists in 30.6 minutes per game this season, and his role only seems to be improving.
Last game, he dished out eight assists against Atlanta as Miami finally recorded its first win of the season. Overall, Larsson has recorded at least four assists in four of five games.
**[Terrence Shannon Jr.](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003230/Terrence+Shannon+Jr.) OVER 14.5 Points (49%)**
Shannon was already primed for a significant role with Minnesota this season, averaging 22.3 minutes and 13.0 points through his first four games. But now he should be even more involved with Edwards out for approximately two months.
In his first game without the former number one overall pick, Shannon played over 27 minutes and scored 19 points against Golden State. His usage rate spiked from 20.7% in the four games with Edwards, up to a whopping 30.6% last game.
Shannon should remain a primary scoring option for Minnesota's second unit, and he should find plenty of minutes with some of Minnesota's highest-usage players off the floor.
**[Ausar Thompson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003036/Ausar+Thompson) OVER 3.5 Assists (57%)**
Pistons head coach J.B. Bickerstaff spoke in the off-season about increased minutes and more on-ball duties for Ausar Thompson this season. The fourth-year wing played more than 33 minutes in his first game before getting injured in the second. He fouled out after 25 minutes in his return from the injury and then played 29 minutes in his last game against the Hornets.
As for the playmaking opportunities, Thompson has recorded at least five assists in three of the four games. Foul trouble will always be a concern for one of the league's top perimeter defenders, but the role and increased minutes Bickerstaff hinted at seem to be in play.
Thompson has a chance to play 30+ minutes again, and his new role as a secondary ball-handler should allow him to easily surpass 3.5 assists.
**[Tristan da Silva](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003200/Tristan+da+Silva) OVER 11.5 Points (57%)**
[Paolo Banchero](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002863/Paolo+Banchero)'s unfortunate injury has opened more opportunities in Orlando. Tristan da Silva has seized the moment, averaging 19.5 points in two games since Banchero went down.
The third-year forward scored 20 points against Toronto earlier this week, even as Orlando put up just 99 total points. Toronto has been the league's best defense on the young season, but they will also be without [Kawhi Leonard](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000646/Kawhi+Leonard) for several weeks.
da Silva is averaging nearly 29 minutes per game, and there is upside for even more while Banchero and [Jalen Suggs](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002670/Jalen+Suggs) are out of the lineup. The absence of those two key players should also mean increased shot volume for da Silva, which gives him a great chance to cruise over his modest points prop.
Good luck if you're tailing, and thanks for reading!

## What is Novig?
Novig is one of the fastest-growing peer-to-peer sports prediction markets. On Novig, users trade directly with each other without the "vig" that is associated with a typical sportsbook.
The vig represents the money that sportsbooks charge you to use their service. It's a hidden cost built into the odds offered on each side of a bet. When a book offers -110 odds on both sides of a bet, they're keeping close to 10% of every bettor's wager, regardless of whether you win or lose.
On [Novig](https://novig.onelink.me/JHQQ/nfg364h9), users trade with each other on the outcomes of sporting events, similarly to the way that investors trade on the stock market. But instead of having to beat the books, you just have to win against other users - there's no commission kept by the site.
Users can bet on most traditional sports markets, including moneyline bets, spreads, totals, player props, parlays, and futures! No politics, no betting on wars...just sports!
## NBA Prop Bet Picks
_All odds are from Novig and were accurate as of 6:00 AM ET. All odds listed are from Novig and shown as percentages, not traditional American odds._
**[Naz Reid](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002336/Naz+Reid) OVER 17.5 Points (52%)**
The Hornets are off to a strong 3-1 start, despite missing two starters as [Kon Knueppel](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003451/Kon+Knueppel) and [Coby White](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002280/Coby+White) have yet to play a game. Both Reid and [Brandon Miller](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003032/Brandon+Miller) have had to carry this offense, and it's led to a career-high 28% usage rate for Reid in his new featured role.
He's a good outside shooter, but can also get buckets in the paint. He's really had his full offensive arsenal on display this year, and has gone for 18 or more points in three of four games, just narrowly missing with 17 points in 25 minutes against the Hawks in a game where he didn't play in the fourth quarter due to a blowout.
He faces the Pistons tonight in the second half of a back-to-back, and Detroit is allowing opposing power forwards to score at a high rate already this year.
**[Anthony Black](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003038/Anthony+Black) OVER 13.5 Points (56%)**
The player we've come to affectionately know as "AB" wasted no time making a big impact for the Magic in his first game of the season, dropping 18 points in 26 minutes against the Knicks.
Black missed the entire preseason and Orlando's first three games, but didn't look rusty at all, shooting 6-for-11 from the field and knocking down five threes. With [Paolo Banchero](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002863/Paolo+Banchero) and [Jalen Suggs](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002670/Jalen+Suggs) both out for a while, he's going to have to take on a big role for this Magic team. He should start tonight, and his points prop is far too low for a player of his caliber.
We saw him break out last year when he was thrust into a similar situation. I expect him to play around 30 minutes and to have a big game here against Toronto in what should be a really good game, and likely a close contest that comes down to the wire.

## NBA Value Props
_All odds are from Novig and were accurate as of 6:00 AM ET. All odds listed are from Novig and shown as percentages, not traditional American odds._
**[Tari Eason](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002883/Tari+Eason) OVER 6.5 Rebounds (49%)**
Eason is likely to move into the starting lineup tonight with both [Alperen Sengun](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002666/Alperen+Seng%C3%BCn) and [Steven Adams](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000875/Steven+Adams) out for the Rockets. Houston will likely shift [Jabari Smith Jr.](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002871/Jabari+Smith+Jr.) to center and insert Eason at the four, where he has a plus matchup against [Kyle Kuzma](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001858/Kyle+Kuzma) and a Milwaukee team that is giving up boards in bunches to bigs.
Eason is coming off an 8-rebound game against the Hawks and played 32 minutes in that one. Last year, he went over this number in six of the eight games that Alperen Sengun missed, with 8 or more in those six games. He's an excellent rebounder, and this game environment should produce plenty of rebounding opportunities.
**[Collin Murray-Boyles](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003454/Collin+Murray-Boyles) OVER 5.5 Rebounds (53%)**
Toronto is set to deal with a multi-week absence of [Kawhi Leonard](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000646/Kawhi+Leonard), and CMB is one of several players who are going to have to step up in a big way.
While I expect others (like [Ja'Kobe Walter](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003197/Ja'Kobe+Walter)) to help pick up the scoring for the Raptors, I also think the second-year big man Murray-Boyles is going to have to step into a larger role, too. He's yet to play 20 minutes this season, but his coach had talked about using him at both power forward and center this year, and resuming a sixth-man type role for Toronto.
I think his low minutes have more to do with Toronto being in a few blowouts early this year and with Walter shifting into the starting lineup; look for CMB to now play closer to 24-25 minutes per night. He's a low-usage player, but a tenacious rebounder who already has 6+ boards in two out of the Raptors' three games.
If we get 24 minutes for CMB tonight, I think he could easily grab as many as 10 boards in a good matchup with an undersized Orlando frontcourt that is allowing the most boards to opposing power forwards in the NBA so far.
**[Obi Toppin](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002533/Obi+Toppin) OVER 11.5 Points (54%)**
I love targeting bench players who have significant roles in their team's current rotation, and Toppin certainly fits the bill there. He's playing 23 minutes a night off the bench, but has been a nice source of offense for Indiana, averaging 15.8 points per game with a 20% usage rate.
Indiana will be without starting center [Ivica Zubac](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001694/Ivica+Zubac) again in this game tonight, which bodes well for Toppin's playing time as Zubac's replacement at center, [Jay Huff](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002735/Jay+Huff), simply rarely plays as many minutes as a typical starter, even when thrust into the starting lineup. Toppin can play minutes at the four, or some small-ball backup center minutes, and I expect him to continue to get up shots at a high rate.
He's gone over this number in all four games this season, and the Pacers will surely need his offense in this matchup with the Super Sixers.
**[Jaylon Tyson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003233/Jaylon+Tyson) OVER 16.5 Points + Rebounds + Assists (52%)**
Those who have followed my work know that I am a Cavs fan and also a huge fan of Tyson's game. He was a free square for us last season when he filled in as a part-time starter when the Cavs dealt with a bevy of injuries.
He's back to the bench this season, but is the first guy to sub in for Cleveland and is playing around 21 minutes a night. He's anchoring a very good second unit that also features sharpshooter [Sam Merrill](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002585/Sam+Merrill), EuroLeague veteran [Mario Hezonja](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001403/Mario+Hezonja), and rookie bucket-getter [Meleek Thomas](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003730/Meleek+Thomas).
Tyson can do a little of everything, and has already hit 20 PRA in his last two games. In a good matchup with Washington here tonight, I like him to keep up that solid production.
Good luck if you're tailing, and thanks for reading!

Thunder Dan Palyo locks in his top NBA player props and best bets for Friday, 10/23/26. Capitalize on commission-free odds and market edges with today's top Novig picks. 
We head into tonight's 11-game slate with every NBA team now having at least one game under their belt. It would be easy to chase some of the biggest performances from players in their first game, but probably not wise. In a perfect world, I'd love to wait for a solid 5-game sample size before really making any predictions on player props, but who wants to wait that long? I want some action on these games tonight, so I spent some time digging into the spots that I feel are the safest to target in the player prop market on Novig.

## What is Novig?
Novig is one of the fastest-growing peer-to-peer sports prediction markets. On Novig, users trade directly with each other without the "vig" that is associated with a typical sportsbook.
The vig represents the money that sportsbooks charge you to use their service. It's a hidden cost that can be found in the odds that are being offered on each side of a specific bet. When a book offers -110 odds on both sides of a bet, they're keeping close to 10% of every bettor's wager, regardless of whether you win or lose.
On [Novig](https://novig.onelink.me/JHQQ/nfg364h9), users trade with each other on the outcomes of sporting events, similarly to the way that investors trade on the stock market. But instead of having to beat the books, you just have to win against other users - there's no commission kept by the site.
Users can bet on most traditional sports markets, including moneyline bets, spreads, totals, player props, parlays, and futures! No politics, no betting on wars...just sports!
## NBA Prop Bet Picks
_All odds are from Novig and were accurate as of 6:00 AM ET. All odds listed are from Novig and shown as percentages, not traditional American odds._
**[Moussa Diabate](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002903/Moussa+Diabat%C3%A9) OVER 18.5 Points + Rebounds (52%)**
The Charlotte center had an impressive opening night with 17 points and eight rebounds as the Hornets picked up a win over the Nets.
His energy on defense and ability to pound the boards is something that the Hornets need badly, and his 33 minutes in the opener is a great sign that he's really put some distance between himself and backup center [Ryan Kalkbrenner](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003468/Ryan+Kalkbrenner).
Last season when he got 32 minutes or more, he went for 19 or more points and rebounds 75% of the time (12 times in 16 games). Opposing centers have done this in 9 of Atlanta's last 10 games, too, including 22 points and 7 boards from another undersized center, [Wendell Carter Jr.](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001996/Wendell+Carter+Jr.), in the Hawks' opener.
**[Paolo Banchero](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002863/Paolo+Banchero) OVER 5.5 Assists (47%)**
Welcome to the point-Paolo era in Orlando. The sometimes mercurial, but ultra-talented point forward of the Magic dropped 10 dimes in Orlando's loss to Atlanta on opening night.
He looked like the best version of himself, something we haven't really seen from him in a while. It was no secret that he wanted Jahmal Mosley out as coach last season, and he got his wish as he was replaced by Sean Sweeney in the offseason.
Sweeney and Paolo have reportedly hit it off so far, and Sweeney has handed Paolo the keys to the offense. Banchero had 14 potential assists in the opener, and only one player who had that many potential assists (passes that led directly to shots for teammates) had fewer than six assists in their first game.
It also helps that Banchero played 41 minutes on Wednesday. With a lot of players having their minutes held in check for the first few weeks, it appears that Sweeney has no problem keeping his best player on the court for the vast majority of the game. This game has a 1.5-point spread and should be one of the most competitive of the evening, so I expect Banchero to pile up counting stats and play a ton of minutes again.
**[Tyrese Haliburton](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002537/Tyrese+Haliburton) OVER 9.5 Assists (50%)**
It was great to see Haliburton in top form in his first regular season game in over a year. The Pacers' floor general had 23 points and 12 assists in their opening night win against the Pelicans. Those 12 assists came on a league-leading 20 potential assists, so he could have easily had a few more, too.
Haliburton is one of the best passers in the game, and Indiana added an excellent pick-and-roll partner for him last year when they acquired [Ivica Zubac](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001694/Ivica+Zubac) from the Clippers. The Pacers have shooters and finishers surrounding Haliburton, and I won't be surprised if he averaged 10+ dimes per game this season.
The Mavs are a good target tonight, too. They allowed [Fred VanVleet](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001733/Fred+VanVleet) to stack up 11 dimes in their opening night loss to Houston.

## NBA Value Props
_All odds are from Novig and were accurate as of 6:00 AM ET. All odds listed are from Novig and shown as percentages, not traditional American odds._
**[Ausar Thompson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003036/Ausar+Thompson) OVER 6.5 Rebounds (52%)**
Another player who fantasy managers had to be thrilled to see get some solid run in their first game was Ausar Thompson. He played only around 25 minutes per game last season, but is a monster on the boards and one of the best defenders in the league. He played 33 minutes in Detroit's win over Boston on opening day and rolled up 11 boards in the process.
Last year, when he played 30+ minutes, he averaged right around 7 boards per game. The early signs here are that he's earned a bigger role this year, and his rebounds are the most projectable of his stats, even though he's also a good secondary shot-creator on offense, too.
**[Grayson Allen](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001986/Grayson+Allen) OVER 2.5 Three Pointers Made (54%)**
I expect Allen to have another big role tonight for Charlotte, even coming off the bench. He played 29 minutes in the opener and finished with 21 points, while drilling five of his eight three-point attempts.
Allen is one of the best shooters from distance in the league, and Charlotte really needs his offense to start the year as both [Kon Knueppel](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003451/Kon+Knueppel) and [Coby White](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002280/Coby+White) are out. Expect him to be firing away again tonight, and remember that he averaged 3.0 made threes per game last year across 28 minutes per contest.
**[Walker Kessler](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002889/Walker+Kessler) OVER 14.5 Points (56%)**
This bet feels like a slam dunk! Speaking of dunks, Kessler had ten (yes, TEN) of them in his Laker debut, finishing a perfect 12-for-12 from the field for 26 points in just 29 minutes.
Kessler was the beneficiary of good looks from both [Luka Doncic](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001984/Luka+Don%C4%8Di%C4%87) and [Austin Reaves](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002720/Austin+Reaves) all night against the Warriors, and I expect him to pad his stats with easy lob dunks all season. He's also a tenacious rebounder who can snag offensive boards and rack up points on putbacks.
The Clippers allowed [Domantas Sabonis](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001683/Domantas+Sabonis) to shoot 5-for-5 on opening night, and they'll struggle to match up with Kessler's size and physicality in this one. This number is already juiced a bit, but I am comfortable betting it up higher, and I think it's an excellent ladder candidate up to about 20 points!
Good luck if you're tailing, and thanks for reading!
